TestCheck_WithMockRDAPIntegration tests Check with a properly mocked RDAP server.\n588\t// This test injects a mock RDAP server into the bootstrap to test the full integration.\n589\tfunc TestCheck_WithMockRDAPIntegration(t *testing.T) {\n590\t\t// Create a mock RDAP server that returns simple responses.\n591\t\tserver := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n592\t\t\tdomain := strings.TrimPrefix(r.URL.Path, \"/\")\n593\t\n594\t\t\tif strings.Contains(domain, \"available\") {\n595\t\t\t\t// Return 404 for available domains.\n596\t\t\t\tw.WriteHeader(http.StatusNotFound)\n597\t\t\t\treturn\n598\t\t\t}\n599\t\n600\t\t\tif strings.Contains(domain, \"error\") {\n601\t\t\t\t// Return 500 for error domains.\n602\t\t\t\tw.WriteHeader(http.StatusInternalServerError)\n603\t\t\t\treturn\n604\t\t\t}\n605\t\n606\t\t\t// Return 200 with RDAP response for registered domains.\n607\t\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/rdap+json\")\n608\t\t\tw.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)\n609\t\t\tjsonResponse := `{\n610\t\t\t\t\"ldhName\": \"` + domain + `\",\n611\t\t\t\t\"handle\": \"123\",\n612\t\t\t\t\"status\": [\"active\"],\n613\t\t\t\t\"events\": [\n614\t\t\t\t\t{\"eventAction\": \"registration\", \"eventDate\": \"2020-01-01T00:00:00Z\"}\n615\t\t\t\t]\n616\t\t\t}`\n617\t\t\tw.Write([]byte(jsonResponse))\n618\t\t}))\n619\t\tdefer server.Close()\n620\t\n621\t\t// Create a minimal bootstrap with our mock server.\n622\t\tbootstrap, err := checker.NewBootstrapManager(context.Background(), \"\")\n623\t\tif err != nil {\n624\t\t\tt.Skipf(\"skipping test: failed to create bootstrap: %v\", err)\n625\t\t}\n626\t\tdefer bootstrap.Stop()\n627\t\n628\t\t// Manually inject our mock server into the bootstrap.\n629\t\tbootstrap.InjectServers(map[string]string{\n630\t\t\t\"com\": server.URL,\n631\t\t\t\"org\": server.URL,\n632\t\t\t\"dev\": server.URL,\n633\t\t})\n634\t\n635\t\t// Create an RDAP client with our bootstrap.\n636\t\thttpClient := &http.Client{Timeout: 5 * bootstrap,\n642\t\t\tRateLimit:  rateLimiter,\n643\t\t\tUserAgent:  \"test\",\n644\t\t})\n645\t\n646\t\t// Create a checker with our RDAP client.\n647\t\tchk := checker.NewChecker(checker.CheckerConfig{\n648\t\t\tRDAPClient: rdapClient,\n649\t\t\tBootstrap:  bootstrap,\n650\t\t})\n651\t\n652\t\t// Test available domain.\n653\t\tresult, err := chk.Check(context.Background(), \"available.com\")\n654\t\tif err != nil {\n655\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected no error for available.com, got %v\", err)\n656\t\t}\n657\t\tif !result.Available {\n658\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected available.com to be available, got %v\", result.Available)\n659\t\t}\n660\t\n661\t\t// Test taken domain.\n662\t\tresult, err = chk.Check(context.Background(), \"taken.com\")\n663\t\tif err != nil {\n664\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected no error for taken.com, got %v\", err)\n665\t\t}\n666\t\tif result.Available {\n667\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected taken.com to be taken, got %v\", result.Available)\n668\t\t}\n669\t\n670\t\t// Test error domain.\n671\t\tresult, err = chk.Check(context.Background(), \"error.com\")\n672\t\t// The RDAP client returns a result with an Error field set, not a Go error\n673\t\t// So err may be nil but result.Error should be non-empty\n674\t\tif err != nil && result.Error == \"\" {\n675\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected result.Error or err to be set for error.com, got err=%v, result.Error=%q\", err, result.Error)\n676\t\t}\n677\t\tif err == nil && result.Error == \"\" {\n678\t\t\tt.Error(\"expected error.com to have an error in result.Error or return err, got both empty\")\n679\t\t}\n680\t}\n681\t\n682\t// TestCheck_MultiTLDWithMockRDAP tests multi-TLD mode with a mocked RDAP server.\n683\tfunc TestCheck_MultiTLDWithMockRDAP(t *testing.T) {\n684\t\t// Create a mock RDAP server.\n685\t\tserver := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request)
